5a1(1): @#//rnam par dag pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' tsho rtag tu ting nge 'dzin dang /byang chub sems pa' shin tu blo gros dang /byang chub sems 
5a2(1): dpa' brtson 'grus brtan dang /byang chub sems de nas nam mkha' bzhin dang /byang chub sems/dpa' smon lam chen po mi gtong ba dang /byang chub sems ba'i smin sbyin dang /byang chub sems
5a3(1): dpa' nyon mongs nad sel dang /byang chub sems sems dpa' sman gyi rgyal po dang /byang chub sems spong ba'i rgyal 
5a4(1): po dang /byang chub sems sa dpa' lung bstan mchog thob dang /byang chub sems 'da' sprin chen rnams dag pod dang /byang chub sems gda' sprin chen pa chos 'dzin dang /
5a5(1): byang chub sems dpa' sprin chen grags dga' dang /byang chub sems rnams dpa' sprin chen gras pa mtha' med ston dang /byang chub sems ni spong ga sgra dang /
5a6(1): byang chub sems spong spong ga sgra dang /byang chub sems dpa' spong chen bkra shis dang /byang chub sems pa' sprin chen dpal dang /byang chub sems
5b1(1): dpa' sprin chen nyi ma'i snying po dang /byang chub sems dpa' sprin chen zla ba'i snying po dang /byang chubasems dpa' sprin chen skar ma'i 'od dang /byang chub sems dba'
5b2(1): sprin chen me'i 'od dang /byang chubasems dang /sprin chen glog gi 'od dang /byang chub sem dpa' sprin chen 'brug sgra dang /byang chub sems pa' sprin chen 
5b3(1): shes rab kun tu 'char dang /byang chub sems dang /sprin chen rnam dag char pa'i rgyal po dang /byang chub semasadapa' sprin chen me tog shing rgyal dang /byang chub 
5b4(1): sems dpa' sprin chen ut+pala'i dri dang /byang chub sems dada' sprin chen tsan dan dri bsil sku dang /byang chub sems dpa' sprin chen mun sel dang /byang chub 
5b5(1): sem dpa' sprin chen rab rib sel ba lasogs pa/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po tshad med grangs med pa rnams so so nas phyi dri'i dus kyi tshe/
5b6(1): nang du yang dag 'jog pa la langs te/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la spyi bos phyag 'tshal te/lan gsum bskor ba byas
6a1(1): @#//nas phyogs gcig tu 'khod do//de nas yang li tsa byi gzhon nu rnams la 'di lta ste/gzhonu seng ge'i 'od dang /gzho nu seng ge blo gros dang /gzho nu chos sbyin   
6a2(1): dang ;gzho nu dbang po sbyin dang /gzho nu 'od chen dang /gzho nu dpal chen dang /gzho nu sangs rgyas skyong dang /gzho nu chos skyong dang /gzho nu dge 'dun skyong dang /gzho nu rdo rje skyong dang /
6a3(1): gzho nu namakha' skyong dang /gzho nu namakha' sgrogs dang /rin chen snying po gzho nu dang /bkra shis mchog gi snying po gzhonu la sogs pa lnga 'bum brgyad stong de dag kyang /bla na med pa yang 
6a4(1): dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub la bde bar gnas pa/theg pa chen po la rab tu mos po de dag so so nas phyi dro'i dus kyi tshe/bcom ldan 'das gal te der lhags nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhab+sa
6a5(1): la spyi bos phyag 'tshal te/lan gsum bskor ba byas nas phyogs gcig tu 'khod do//gzhan yang lha'i sras po la 'di lta ste/lha'i sras po dga' ba mthong dang /lha'i sras po zab dga' 
6a6(1): dang ;lha'i sras po nyi 'od dang /lha'i sras po zla ba'i gtsug phud dang /lha'i sras po shes rab gsal ba dang /lha'i sras po nam mkha' rnam dag shes rab dang /lha'i sras po nyon mongs
6a7(1): sel dang /lha'i dbang po bkra shis dang /de dag la sogs pa lha'i sras po bzhi khri nyi stong yang theg pa chen po bsrungs pa dang /yang dag pa'i chos rjes su 'dzin cing /rgyun mi 'chad pa'i smon
6b1(1): lam Irgya chen po btab pa de dag thams cad kyang phyi dro'i dus kyi tshe/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der lhags nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la sbyi bos phyag 'tshal te/lan gsum bskor ba  
6b2(1): byas nas/phyogs gcig tu 'khod do//gzhan yang klu'i rgyal po la 'di lta ste/klu'i rgyal po pad+ma dang /klu'i rgyal po e la'i 'dab ma dang /klu'i rgyal po stobs chen dang /
6b3(1): klu'i rgyal po sgra chen dang /klu'i rgyal po rba rlabs chung dang /klu'i rgyal po klung 'dzin dang /klu'i rgyal po gser gtong dang /klu'i rgyal po gzi can la sogs pa klu'i rgyal po nyi khri brgyad stong pa 
6b4(1): de dag kyang thig pa chen po'i chos 'chang zhing 'dzin par mos pa dang /yongs su rgyas pa dang /bsrungspar sems bskyed pa sha stag ste/de dag thams cad kyang so so nas phyi dro'i dus 
6b5(1): kyi tshe bcom ldan 'das ga la bder lhags nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la spyi bos phyag 'tshal te/lan gsum bskor ba byas nas/phyogs gcig tu 'khod do//gzhan 
6b6(1): yang gnod spyin gyi rgyal po rnam thos kyi bu la sogs pa sum khri drug stong la 'di lta ste/gnod sbyin a mra dang /gnod sbyin a mra 'chang dang /gnod sbyin pad+ma 'od kyi snying po 'a dang /gnod 
6b7(1): sbyin pad+mo'i gdong dang /gnod sbyin khro gnyer can dang /gnod sbyin 'jigsa pa chen po ston dang /gnod sbyin sa g.yo byed dang /gnod sbyin zas 'phrog la sogs pa'i gnod sbyin de dag 
7a1(1): @#//kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos yang dag pa la dad cing bsrung ba brtson pa sha stag ste/de dag thams cad kyang phyi dro'i dus kyi tshe/bcom ldan 'das ga la la ba der lhags
7a2(1): nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la sbyi bos phyag 'tshal te/lan gsum bskor ba byas nas phyogs tu 'khod do//gzhan yang nam mkha' lding gi rgyal po spos kyi glang po che mthu stobs rgyal
7a3(1): po la sogs pa bzhi khri dgu stong dang /gzhan yang dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /mi 'am ci dang /lto phye chen po la sogs pa dang /ri dang nags tshal dang /chu bo dang mi la sogs pa de dag thams
7a4(1): cad kyang bla na med pa theg pa chen po 'di klog cing 'don pa dang 'chang zhin 'dzin pa dang /'dri ba dang /rgyas par byed pa dang /bsrungs zhing skyob par smon lam yongs su 'debs pa de dag thams cad
7a5(1): kyang phyi dro'i dus kyi tshe/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der lhags te bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs pa sbyi bos phyag 'tshal te//lan gsum bskor ba byas na/phyogs gcig tu 'khod do//de ltar nyan
7a6(1): thos dang /byang chub sems dpa' dang /lha dang mi dang nam mkha' lding la sogs pa sde brgyad po de dag sprin bzhi du 'dus pa/sems rtse gcig tu byas nas/thal mo sbyar te mig mi 'dzums
7a7(1): pas;bcom ldan 'das kyi zhal du lta zhing /dam pa'i chos bstan pa'i mos pas gsol ba 'debs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das phyi dro'i dus kyi tshe/ting nge 'dzin las bzhengs te/'khor
7b1(1): 'dus pa mang po la gzigs nas tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so//mdo sde'i rgyal po'i dbang po dang //mnyan na shin tu zab pa dang //yongs su brtags na zab pa nyid//gser 'od dam 
7b2(1): pa 'di 'chad do//phyogs bzhir sangs rgyas rnams kyis ni//byin gyis rlabs kyis byin brlabs pa//mi 'khrugs pas ni shar phyogs su//rin chen tog gis lho phyogs su//'od dpag med
7b3(1): kyi nub phyogs su//rnga sgra yis ni byang phyogs su//sdig pa thams cad gzhig pa'i phyir//bshad pa dam pa bkra shis pa//sdig pa'i las rnams zab byed pa//bde ba thams cad sbyin byed pa/
7b4(1): sdug bsngal thams cad 'jig byed pa//thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi gzhi//dpal kun gyis ni legs brgyan pa//byin gyis rlabs te brjod par bya//sems can gang dag dbang po nyams/
7b5(1): tshe zad pa dang tshe nyams dang //pun sum tshogs min gang chags dang //lha rnams rgyab kyis phyogs pa dang //mdza' sdu bskye bo la sogs gtses pa dang //phan tshun mthun pa ma yin dang //nor
7b6(1): bsgribs pas ni gnod pa dang //mya ngan mngalas gnod pa dang //'jigs pa dang ni 'phongs pa dang //gza' dang rgyu skar byang sdems dang //mi bzang gdon gyis nyen pa dang //mya ngan mang la las
7b7(1): byung ba yi//rmi lam sdig pa mthong na yang //des ni rab bkrus gtsang ma yin//mdo sde dam pa mnyam par bya//gang dag yid dge sems dang zhing //gos gtsang rnams kyis rab brgyan te//
8a1(1): @#//zab mo sangs rgyas spyod yul ba//mdo sde 'di ni nyan byed na//mdo sde 'di yi gzi brjid kyis//srog chags kun gyi gnod pa dag/shin tu mi bzad 'di 'dra ba//de dag tu 
8a2(1): rtag tu zhi bar 'gyur//'jig rten skyong ba bdag nyid dang //blon po'i tshigs kyi dbang phyug bcas//gnod sbyin bye ba du ma yis//de dag la ni srung bar byed//lha mo chen mo dbyangs can dang /nai rany+dza
8a3(1): nar gnas pa dang //'byung po ma mo 'phrog pa dang //sa'i lha mo brtan ma dang //tshangs pa'i dbang dang lha dbang dang //klu dbang mthu chen rnams dang ni//mi'am ci dbang lha min dbang //de bzhin nam
8a4(1): mkha' lding dbang po//sde dang stogs dang bzhon pa dang //bcas te de dag der lhags nas//nyin dang mtshan mo mi g.yel bar//de dag la ni srung par byed//zab mo sangs rgyas spyod yul ba//sangs
8a5(1): rgyas kun gyi gsang pa ste//bskal pa bye ba rnyed dka' ba//mdo 'di rab tu bshad par bya//gang dag mdo 'di nyan par dang //gang dag gzhan la thos byed dang //gang dag rjes su yi rang dang //gang
8a6(1): dag mchod pa byed pa rnams//de dag lha dang klu dag dang //mi dang mi 'am ci dag dang //lha min gnod sbyin du ma yis//bskal pa bye bar mchod par 'gyur//srog chags bsod nams byed pa rnams
8a7(1): de dag bsod nams phung po ni//mtha' yas grangs kyang med pa dang /bsam gyis mi khyab skye bar 'gyur//phyogs bcu'i rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas dang //de bzhin zab mo spyod pa yi//byang chub sems
8b1(1): dpa' rnams kyis kyang //rab tu bzung bar 'gyur ba yin//gtsang ma'i gos rnams rab bgos la//dri zhim pa'i spos 'chang zhing //byams pa'i sems ni nyer gzhag nas//g.yel ba med pas  
8b2(1): mchod par byas//rang gi sems ni dri med cing //rgya che bar yang rab byed la//sems kyang shin tu dang byed kyis//mdo sde dam pa mnyan par bgyi//gang dag mdo sde 'di nyan pa//de dag mi ngan leg+sa 
8b3(1): par 'ongs//mi yi srid pa lags par thob//'tsho ba bde bas 'tsho bar 'gyur//gang gi rna ba'i bu gar yang //bshad pa 'di ni grangs 'gyur ba//de dag dge rtsa spyangs pa ste//sangs rgyas mang 
8b4(1): pos rab tu bsngags////gser 'od dam pa mdo sde'i dbang po'i rgyal po las//gleng gzhi'i le'u zhes bya ste dang pa'o////de'i tshe de'i dus na rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer
8b5(1): chen po na/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po mdzes pa'i tog ces bya ba/sngon gyi rgyal ba la lhag par bya ba byas pa/dge ba'i rtsa ba bskyed pa//sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag 
8b6(1): mang po la//rim gro byas pa zhig gnas pa de 'di snyam du sems te/rgyu gang dang rkyen gang gis bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa 'di lta ste/lo brgyad cu zhig thub ste 'di ltar sku tshe thung snyam nas de yang  
8b7(1): 'di snyam du sems te/bcom ldan 'das kyis kyang 'di skad du rgyu gnyis dang /rkyen gnyis kyis tshe ring bar 'gyur te/gnyis gang zhe na/'di lta ste/srog gcod pa spangs pa dang /kha zas rab tu  
9a1(1): @#//sbyin pa'o//zhes gsungs te/bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pas ni/bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong grangs med pa mang po nas/srog gcod pa spangs par   
9a2(1): gyur pas na/dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam kyi bar du yang dag par blangs te gnas par gyur/zas kyang phyi nang gi dngos po rnams sems can rnam dag la yongs su btang ste/cher na rang gi 
9a3(1): lus kyi sha dang khrag dang /rus ba dang /rkang gis kyang sems can ltogs pa rnams tshim par mdzad na//zas gzhan gyi lta ci smos snyam mo//de nas skyes bu dam pa de sangs rgyas la song ba'i yid 
9a4(1): la byed pa dag las 'di lta ba'i tshul gyis bsam pa 'di dag sems ni/de'i khyim yangs shing rgya che la/bai dUr+ya sngon po las grub pa/lha'i rin po che du mas spras pa/de bzhin gshegs pas 
9a5(1): kha dog bsgyur ba/lha las 'das pa'i spos kyis khyab par gyur to//khyim de'i phyogs bzhi nas lha'i rin po che'i khri bzhi byung bar gyur to//khri de dag las kyang lha'i khri lha'i ris bcos bu  
9a6(1): rin po che'i phrugs rnams bting ba dag byung bar gyur to//khrid dag las lha'i me tog pad+ma rin po che du mas spras pa /de bzhin gshegs pas kha dog bsgyur ba dag byung ngo //pad+ma de dag 
9a7(1): las kyang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das bzhi byung bar gyur to/shar phyogs nas ni de bzhin gshegs pa mi 'khrugs pa byung /lho phyogs nas ni de bzhin gshags pa rin chen tog byung /
9b1(1): nub phyogs nas ni de bzhin gshegs pa tshe dpag med byung /byang phyogs nas ni de bzhin gshegs pa rnga sgra byung ste/seng ge'i khri de dag las sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag byung ma thag 
9b2(1): tu;;de nas de'i tshe rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer chen po snang ba chen pos khyab bar gyur to//stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi bar dang /phyogs bcu kun tu 'jig rten 
9b3(1): gyi khams gang+gA'i klung gyi bye ma snyed kyi bar dag snang ba chen pos khyab par gyur te/lha'i me tog gi char yang 'bab lha'i bsil snyan dag gi sgra yang byung ngo//sangs rgyas kyi mthus stong gsum 
9b4(1): gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rtenagyi khams 'dir sems can thams cad kyang lha'i bde ba dang ldan par 'gyur to//sems can dbang po ma tshang ba rnams ni dbang po thams cad dang ldan bar gyur 
9b5(1): sems can dmus long dag ni mig gis gzugs mthong bar 'gyur/sems can rna ba 'on pa dag ni rna bas sgra thos/sems can myos pa rnams ni dran pa so sor rnyed/sems can sem+sa 
9b6(1): rnams par g.yengs pa dang ni sems rnam par mi g.yeng bar 'gyur/sems canasgren mo rnams ni gos bgos par 'gyur/sems can bkres pa dag ni lto 'drangs par 'gyur/sems 
9b7(1): can skom pa dag ni skom pa dang bral bar gyur/sems can nad kyis btab pa dag ni nad dang bral bar gyur/lus kyi dbang po nyams pa ni dbang po tshang bar gyur te/'jig rten du ngo mtshar rmad 
10a1(1): @#//du byung ba'i chos rnams rgya cher byung bar gyur to//de nas byang chub sems dpa' mdzes pa'i dog gis sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag mthong nas ngo mtshar rmad du gyur 
10a2(1): te|de tshim zhing mgu la yi rangs shing rab tu dga' ste/dga' ba dang yi bde ba bskyes nas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasade dag ga la ba der logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud nas/sangs 
10a3(1): rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dang rnam rjes su dran la/bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa'i yon tan rjes su dran zhing bcom ldan 'das shAkya thu ba pa 'di'i sku tshe'i tshad la the tshom za bar gyur to//bcom ldan 
10a4(1): 'das shAkya thub pa 'di lta na lo brgyad cu zhig thub ste/sku tshe thung ba 'di ji lta bu/'di ci zhig snyam du sems shing 'dug par gyur to//de nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'as de dag dgongs shing mkhyen 
10a5(1): bzhi na du byang chub sems dpa' mdzas pa'i tog de la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rigs kyi bu khyod 'di lta snyam du/bcom ldan 'das shAkya thab pa 'di lta bu sku tshe thung ngo snyam du ma sems 
10a6(1): shig/de ci'i phyir zhe na/rigs kyi bu de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams ma gtogs par lha dang bcas pa/bdud dang bcas pa/tshangs pa dang 
10a7(1): bcas pa/dge sbyong dang bram ze'i skyed rgu dang bcas pa'i lha dang /mi dang lha ma yin du bcas pa na/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub pa'i sku tshe'i tshad kyi mtha' phyi ma'i 
10b1(1): mtha'i mu'i bar du yang ma gtogs pa nus pa deng kyis su yang ma mthong ngo //sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku tshe'i tshad bshad pa brjod ma thag tu/de nas de'i 
10b2(1): mod la sangs rgyas kyi mthus lha'i bu 'dod pa na spyod pa rnams dang /gzugs na spyod pa rnams dang /klu dang gnod spyin dang /dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /mi 'am ci dang /

10b4(1): gshegs pa de dag gis thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor de la/bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa'i sku tshe'i tshad bshad pachigs su bcad de mdor bsdus te bka' stsal pa/rgya mtsho'i chu rnams tham+sa 
10b5(1): kyi;/thigs pa dag ni bgrang bar nus//shAkya thub pa'i sku tshe'i tshad//sus kyang bgrang bar mi nus so//ri rab rnams ni thams cad kyang //phra rab rdul blags bgrang bar nus//shAkya
10b6(1): thub pa'i sku tshe'i tshad //sus kyang bgrang bar minus so//(sa)la gang dag yod pa yi//rab tu phra rdul ji snyed pa//thams cad bgrang bar nus srid kyi//rgyal ba'i sku tshe'i tshad mi nus//gal 
10b7(1): te la las nam mkha' la//sna 'ga' zhig gis dpag 'dod kyang //shAkya thub pa'i sku tshe 'i tshad//sus kyang bgrang par mi nus so//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'di srid bzhugs//bskal    
11a1(1): @#//pa bye ba dag ni 'di snying cig/bskal pa bye ba brgya dag ces//grangs ni rnyed par mi rung ngo //gang phyir de yi byed rgyu gnyis//rgyu yang rnam pa gnyis gsungs te//
11a2(1): gzhan la 'tshe ba spangs pa dang //kha zas mang po byin pa ste//de'i phyi ba dag nyid che ba bye'i//sku tshe'i grangs ni mi rnyed do//bskal pa 'di snyed gcig ce 'am//grangs med pa yang de bzhin
11a3(1): te;;de bas rgyal ba'i sku tshe'i tshad//the tshom cung zad ma za zhig/rgyal ba'i sku tshe'i tshamatha' ma yi//grangs ni gang du 'ang mi dmigs so//de nas byang chub sems dpa' mdzes pa'i tog g+si
11a4(1): bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa'i sku tshe'i tshad bshad pa thos nas//bcom ldan 'das de dag la 'di skad ces gsol to//ji ltar na bcom ldan 'das shAkya thubapa de bzhin gshegs pas de

11a6(1): stsal to//bcom ldan 'das shAkya thu ba pa de bzhin gshegs pa de ni snyigs ma lnga rnam pa lnga'i 'jig rten du skye dgu'i tshe lo brgya thub pa'i dus su bltas nas/sems can mos pa dman pa/
11a7(1): dge ba'i rtsa ba dang /dad pa chung ba'i sems can de bdag tu blta ba dang /sems can du lta ba dang /srog tu blta ba dang /gso bar blta ba dang /log bar blta ba dang /bdag dang 
11b1(1): bdag gir 'dzin pa dag dang /rtag pa dang chad par lta ba lasogs pa'i byis pa so so'i skye bo rnams la phan 'dogs pa dang /gzhan mu stegs can rnams yang dag pa'i lta ba bskyed pas/
11b2(1): myur du bla na med pa'i byang chub thob par bya ba'i phyir/de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub pa des/sku tshe'i tshad thung ba bstan to//gzhan yang rigs kyi bu de bzhin gshegs pa de mya ngan 'da' 
11b3(1): ba bstan pas/sems can rnams de dkon pa dang phrad par dka' ba'i 'du shes dang /mya ngan dang sdug bsngal gyi 'du shes lasogs pa bskyed pas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyis   
11b4(1): gsungs pa'i mdo 'di dag myur du 'dzin pa dang /klog pa dang /kun chub par byed pa dang /gzhan dag yang rgya cher yang dag par ston pa la bskur pa 'debs pa med par 'gyur pas/
11b5(1): de'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pas sku tshe thung (ba)bstan to//de yang de bzhin gshegs pa rtag tu zhugs shing mya ngan las mi 'da' na/sems can rnams gus pa med pa dang /'phrad par dka' 
11b6(1): ba'i 'du shes kyang mi bskyed pas de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs pa'i mdo zab mo dag 'dzin cing klog pa dang /kun chub bar byed pa dang /gzhan dang las yang rgya cher yang dag paraston par 
11b7(1): mi byed do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni bcom ldan 'das rtag tu 'jig rten de na bzhugs par 'dzin pa'i phyir ro//rigs kyi bu dper na gang la la zhig pha ma nor bu rin po che rgyas pa mang du yod pa mthong
